Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The combinatorics operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 794. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 824 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the combinatorics space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 794 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 824. QED.
